# MyLite ↔ MySQL 8.4.9 compatibility

This document tracks MyLite's first baseline compatibility target against
MySQL 8 LTS, currently MySQL 8.4.9. The baseline tables below are the focused
near-term implementation contract. The complete compatibility inventory remains
in the [detailed compatibility tables](#detailed-compatibility-tables).

## Legend

| Mark | Meaning |
| :-: | --- |
| ✅ | Supported with MySQL 8.4.9-compatible behavior and runtime comparison tests for relevant results, errors, warnings, metadata, affected rows, side effects, and protocol details. |
| 🟡 | Implemented with documented compatibility gaps, reduced fidelity, or MyLite-specific behavior that is covered by tests. |
| ⚪ | Accepted at parse or API level and intentionally handled as an embedded-compatible no-op, placeholder, warning, or diagnostic. |
| ❌ | Not implemented, rejected, or not yet MySQL-runtime verified in MyLite. |

## Baseline Compatibility

Each row tracks one baseline behavior or feature. A row moves out of `❌` only
when the listed surface is implemented and covered by MySQL 8.4.9 comparison
tests.

### Runtime, Identity, and Diagnostics

| Feature | Status | Notes | Full table |
| --- | :-: | --- | --- |
| Dynamic current database | 🟡 | `USE` selects catalog schemas and the synthetic `information_schema`, `mysql`, `performance_schema`, and `sys` schemas; `DATABASE()` / `SCHEMA()` return the selected schema or `NULL` in the limited scalar-select slice; dropping a selected MyLite catalog schema clears the selection. | [SQL schemas](docs/compatibility/sql-schemas.md), [runtime session state](docs/compatibility/runtime-session-sql-modes.md), [system functions](docs/compatibility/functions-system.md), [INFORMATION_SCHEMA tables](docs/compatibility/metadata-information-schema.md) |
| Database listing baseline | 🟡 | Descriptor-driven `SHOW DATABASES` / `SHOW SCHEMAS` for synthetic built-in schemas plus MyLite catalog schemas, with limited queryable `INFORMATION_SCHEMA.SCHEMATA` rows for the same schema names; supports `LIKE` filters but no `WHERE`, privileges, or system tables beyond the documented metadata subset. | [SQL SHOW statements](docs/compatibility/sql-show-statements.md), [INFORMATION_SCHEMA tables](docs/compatibility/metadata-information-schema.md), [baseline built-in schema catalog](docs/specs/baseline-builtin-schema-catalog/specs.md) |
| Built-in schema access semantics | 🟡 | Limited metadata-only system-schema baseline: `USE information_schema`, `USE mysql`, `USE performance_schema`, and `USE sys` succeed, currently supported metadata surfaces can list built-in schema rows, and currently supported schema/table/index/rename/truncate/single-table DML writes targeting any built-in schema fail before catalog mutation. `information_schema` and `performance_schema` use `1044 / 42000`; `mysql` and `sys` use `3552 / HY000` as an explicit stricter embedded-design decision despite MySQL 8.4.9 allowing some `root` temporary-table writes there. No privilege engine, accounts, writable system views, or physical system-schema tables. | [INFORMATION_SCHEMA tables](docs/compatibility/metadata-information-schema.md), [SQL schemas](docs/compatibility/sql-schemas.md), [SQL table DDL](docs/compatibility/sql-table-ddl.md), [SQL table DML](docs/compatibility/sql-table-dml.md), [baseline built-in schema write protection](docs/specs/baseline-builtin-schema-write-protection/specs.md) |
| Current user identity | 🟡 | Limited scalar `USER()`, `SESSION_USER()`, `SYSTEM_USER()`, `CURRENT_USER()`, and bare `CURRENT_USER` expose MyLite's embedded `root@%` session identity; limited `INFORMATION_SCHEMA.USER_PRIVILEGES` and current-user `SHOW GRANTS` expose synthetic global privilege rows/text for that embedded identity, while limited `SHOW PRIVILEGES` exposes the static MySQL 8.4.9 privilege-name catalog; no accounts, authentication, roles, grant descriptors, privilege enforcement, definer semantics, `IGNORE_SPACE`, or stored-function resolution. | [SQL users, roles, and privileges](docs/compatibility/sql-users-privileges.md), [system functions](docs/compatibility/functions-system.md), [INFORMATION_SCHEMA tables](docs/compatibility/metadata-information-schema.md), [SQL SHOW statements](docs/compatibility/sql-show-statements.md) |
| Current role function | 🟡 | Limited scalar `CURRENT_ROLE()` exposes MyLite's no-active-role value `NONE`; no role catalog, role grants, default roles, `SET ROLE`, privileges, or bare `CURRENT_ROLE`. | [SQL users, roles, and privileges](docs/compatibility/sql-users-privileges.md), [system functions](docs/compatibility/functions-system.md) |
| Version function | 🟡 | Limited scalar `VERSION()` exposes the fixed MySQL 8.4.9 compatibility version string; the public `mylite_version()` API remains MyLite's library version, and protocol handshake version reporting is out of scope. | [system functions](docs/compatibility/functions-system.md), [baseline MySQL server version identity](docs/specs/baseline-mysql-server-version-identity/specs.md) |
| Version system variables | 🟡 | Limited scalar reads and `SHOW VARIABLES` rows for `@@version` and `@@version_comment` expose the fixed MySQL 8.4.9 compatibility version and community-server comment; no protocol handshake behavior or configurable version identity, and version compile placeholders are tracked separately. | [runtime system variables](docs/compatibility/runtime-system-variables.md), [system functions](docs/compatibility/functions-system.md), [baseline MySQL server version identity](docs/specs/baseline-mysql-server-version-identity/specs.md) |
| Diagnostics count variables | 🟡 | Limited scalar `SELECT @@warning_count` and `SELECT @@error_count` read the previous diagnostics snapshot and then clear it like MySQL nondiagnostic `SELECT`; supports `session`/`local` qualifiers only. | [runtime system variables](docs/compatibility/runtime-system-variables.md), [error, warning, and result semantics](docs/compatibility/error-warning-result-semantics.md) |
| Explicit defaults for timestamp system variable | 🟡 | Limited scalar reads and `SHOW VARIABLES` rows for `@@explicit_defaults_for_timestamp` expose MyLite's fixed modern MySQL baseline `1` / `ON`; limited fixed no-op `SET` forms may preserve `ON`; no deprecated `OFF` timestamp semantics, mutable state, startup options, or changed temporal DDL/DML behavior. | [runtime system variables](docs/compatibility/runtime-system-variables.md), [baseline explicit defaults for timestamp system variable](docs/specs/baseline-explicit-defaults-for-timestamp-system-variable/specs.md) |

No `ALTER KEY`, optimizer index-use semantics, temporary tables, views, or multi-action forms. | [SQL indexes and constraints](docs/compatibility/sql-indexes-constraints.md) |
| `DISABLE KEYS` / `ENABLE KEYS` | 🟡 | Limited single-action `ALTER TABLE table_name DISABLE KEYS` and `ALTER TABLE table_name ENABLE KEYS` for persistent base tables and shadowing session temporary tables. The operation is an InnoDB-style no-op for current MyLite descriptors: it validates the writable target, preserves rows, index metadata, catalog generation, and SQLite schema generation, reports zero affected rows for non-`COPY` forms or the current target row count for `ALGORITHM=COPY`, and emits MySQL-compatible `Note 1031`. Admits the limited comma-separated `ALGORITHM` / `LOCK` option tail with MySQL-compatible incompatible-lock diagnostics. No MyISAM key maintenance, disabled-index metadata, physical SQLite index changes, optimizer effects, or multi-action `ALTER` forms. | [SQL indexes and constraints](docs/compatibility/sql-indexes-constraints.md), [baseline ALTER TABLE DISABLE/ENABLE KEYS](docs/specs/baseline-alter-table-disable-enable-keys/specs.md) |

No multi-action forms, broader expressions, temporary tables, algorithms, locks, or privilege semantics. | [SQL indexes and constraints](docs/compatibility/sql-indexes-constraints.md) |
| `DROP CHECK` | 🟡 | Limited single-action `ALTER TABLE table_name DROP CHECK check_name` and limited `ALTER TABLE table_name DROP CONSTRAINT check_name` for descriptor-owned CHECK constraints on persistent base tables; removes metadata, rebuilds only when an enforced physical CHECK must be removed, reports zero rows, and updates descriptor-driven metadata. No multi-action forms, temporary tables, algorithms beyond the shared option-tail subset, locks beyond the shared option-tail subset, cascades, or privilege semantics. | [SQL indexes and constraints](docs/compatibility/sql-indexes-constraints.md) |
| `ALTER CHECK ENFORCED` / `NOT ENFORCED` | 🟡 | Limited single-action `ALTER TABLE table_name ALTER CHECK check_name ENFORCED\|NOT ENFORCED` for descriptor-owned CHECK constraints on persistent base tables; enforcing validates existing rows and reports the scan count, unenforcing removes physical enforcement and reports zero rows, and idempotent toggles are metadata no-ops. No `ALTER CONSTRAINT`, multi-action forms, temporary tables, algorithms, locks, or privilege semantics. | [SQL indexes and constraints](docs/compatibility/sql-indexes-constraints.md) |
| Foreign keys | 🟡 | Limited descriptor-owned integer-family foreign keys for persistent base tables through table-level `CREATE TABLE` definitions, named single-action `ALTER TABLE ... ADD CONSTRAINT ... FOREIGN KEY`, and unnamed single-action `ALTER TABLE ... ADD FOREIGN KEY`, including optional FK index symbols, one-column and ordered composite full-column child/reference lists in the same schema, and `CASCADE`, `RESTRICT`, `NO ACTION`, or `SET NULL` action metadata. Parent keys must be exactly matching primary or unique descriptors, child indexes are reused when the leftmost full parts match or auto-created when needed with MySQL-compatible constraint/index-symbol naming for the admitted subset, descriptor-driven DML enforcement uses MySQL `MATCH SIMPLE` NULL-tuple behavior, direct non-recursive `ON DELETE CASCADE` child removal, `ON UPDATE CASCADE` child-key updates, and `ON DELETE` / `ON UPDATE SET NULL` child-key nulling apply to supported parent `DELETE`/`UPDATE` writes without changing parent affected-row counts when `foreign_key_checks` is enabled; disabling the handle-local session variable skips those descriptor DML checks/actions. `SHOW CREATE TABLE` plus limited information-schema metadata render ordered parts and non-default actions; dropping removes only FK descriptors and preserves the child index. Validates existing rows, rejects `SET NULL` over `NOT NULL` child columns, records ordered descriptor metadata, reuses or auto-creates a child index when needed with MySQL-compatible constraint/index-symbol naming for the admitted subset, and admits the limited comma-separated `ALGORITHM` / `LOCK` option tail only for MySQL-compatible `DEFAULT` / `COPY` forms while rejecting `INSTANT`, `INPLACE`, and `LOCK=NONE` under MyLite's fixed enabled foreign-key checks policy. ### SQL Modes

| Feature | Status | Notes | Full table |
| --- | :-: | --- | --- |
| MySQL default SQL modes | 🟡 | Limited scalar `@@sql_mode` reads, fixed global value, and mutable session-local `SET sql_mode` support expose the default 8.4.9 mode set and canonical assigned mode strings; only listed partial effects are implemented. | [runtime session state and SQL modes](docs/compatibility/runtime-session-sql-modes.md), [runtime system variables](docs/compatibility/runtime-system-variables.md) |
| `NO_AUTO_VALUE_ON_ZERO` | 🟡 | Limited effect for current descriptor-owned `AUTO_INCREMENT` `INSERT` paths: explicit `0` stores zero instead of being replaced by a generated value; exact InnoDB-style counter reservation gaps after all prior insert patterns remain outside the current slice. | [runtime session state and SQL modes](docs/compatibility/runtime-session-sql-modes.md) |
| `NO_BACKSLASH_ESCAPES` | 🟡 | Limited lexer/string-decoding effect for later admitted string literals. | [runtime session state and SQL modes](docs/compatibility/runtime-session-sql-modes.md) |
| `NO_ZERO_DATE` | 🟡 | Accepted and reflected in session `@@sql_mode` with verified MySQL warning behavior and limited full-zero canonical and admitted relaxed `DATE` / `DATETIME` / `TIMESTAMP` conversion effects for row values, defaults, supported ALTER default changes, compatible descriptor copies, and predicates. | [runtime session state and SQL modes](docs/compatibility/runtime-session-sql-modes.md) |
| `NO_ZERO_IN_DATE` | 🟡 | Accepted and reflected in session `@@sql_mode` with verified MySQL warning behavior and limited partial-zero canonical and admitted relaxed `DATE` / `DATETIME` conversion effects for row values, defaults, supported ALTER default changes, compatible descriptor copies, and predicates. | [runtime session state and SQL modes](docs/compatibility/runtime-session-sql-modes.md) |
| `ONLY_FULL_GROUP_BY` | 🟡 | Accepted and reflected in session `@@sql_mode`; MyLite currently applies the documented grouped-query validation slices directly, including limited complete-primary-key functional dependence, but does not yet make those checks conditional on this mode or implement broader MySQL `ONLY_FULL_GROUP_BY` semantics. | [runtime session state and SQL modes](docs/compatibility/runtime-session-sql-modes.md) |
| `PIPES_AS_CONCAT` | 🟡 | Accepted and reflected in session `@@sql_mode`; limited mode-sensitive `||` string concatenation is supported for no-source/`DUAL` scalar projection, `DO`, and current single-table row-scalar `SELECT` projection over the existing `CONCAT()` operand envelope. Predicate truth evaluation, DML assignments, ordering/grouping expressions, scalar logical `||` outside this mode, and general expression metadata remain unsupported. | [runtime session state and SQL modes](docs/compatibility/runtime-session-sql-modes.md), [baseline PIPES_AS_CONCAT](docs/specs/baseline-pipes-as-concat/specs.md) |
| `STRICT_ALL_TABLES` | 🟡 | Accepted and reflected in session `@@sql_mode`; limited strict conversion effects exist for the current zero-temporal `DATE` / `DATETIME` / `TIMESTAMP` subset and ordinary DML missing-default / matched-update `NULL` and `DEFAULT` handling, while broader strict conversion remains unsupported. | [runtime session state and SQL modes](docs/compatibility/runtime-session-sql-modes.md) |
| `STRICT_TRANS_TABLES` | 🟡 | Accepted and reflected in session `@@sql_mode` with verified MySQL warning behavior; limited strict conversion effects exist for the current zero-temporal `DATE` / `DATETIME` / `TIMESTAMP` subset and ordinary DML missing-default / matched-update `NULL` and `DEFAULT` handling, while broader strict conversion remains unsupported. | [runtime session state and SQL modes](docs/compatibility/runtime-session-sql-modes.md) |
| `ANSI` | 🟡 | Accepted as a composite session mode; its `ANSI_QUOTES`, `REAL_AS_FLOAT`, and limited `PIPES_AS_CONCAT` effects are currently observable. | [runtime session state and SQL modes](docs/compatibility/runtime-session-sql-modes.md), [baseline PIPES_AS_CONCAT](docs/specs/baseline-pipes-as-concat/specs.md) |
| `ANSI_QUOTES` | 🟡 | Limited lexer effect for later double-quoted identifiers. | [runtime session state and SQL modes](docs/compatibility/runtime-session-sql-modes.md) |
| `REAL_AS_FLOAT` | 🟡 | Limited DDL effect for later `REAL` column definitions, which map to MyLite's `FLOAT` descriptor subset. | [runtime session state and SQL modes](docs/compatibility/runtime-session-sql-modes.md) |

| `JSON_CONTAINS()` | 🟡 | Limited no-source/`DUAL`/`DO` and single-table row-scalar projection/predicate containment over SQL string/`NULL`, JSON descriptor, and nonbinary string descriptor document/candidate values, with optional simple path string/`NULL` literal and current signed-integer JSON number subset; no wildcard/range paths, arbitrary expression arguments, binary strings, decimals/exponents, ordering/grouping expressions, DML assignment expressions, mutation, or protocol metadata beyond limited scalar/row-scalar result-column metadata. | [JSON functions and operators](docs/compatibility/functions-json.md) |
| `JSON_CONTAINS_PATH()` | 🟡 | Limited no-source/`DUAL`/`DO` and single-table row-scalar projection/predicate path existence over SQL string/`NULL`, JSON descriptor, and nonbinary string descriptor document values with case-insensitive literal `one`/`all` and simple path string/`NULL` literals; no wildcard/range/multi-match paths, arbitrary expression arguments, path columns, binary strings, ordering/grouping expressions, DML assignment expressions, or protocol metadata beyond limited scalar/row-scalar result-column metadata. | [JSON functions and operators](docs/compatibility/functions-json.md) |
| `JSON_EXTRACT()` | 🟡 | Limited no-source/`DUAL`/`DO` and single-table row-scalar projection with one JSON document operand and one simple path string literal; supports SQL string literals, `NULL`, JSON columns, and nonbinary string columns, returning JSON text or SQL `NULL`; no multiple paths, wildcards/ranges, predicates, expression ordering, DML assignment expressions, arbitrary expressions, or protocol metadata beyond limited scalar/row-scalar result-column metadata. | [JSON functions and operators](docs/compatibility/functions-json.md) |
| `JSON_INSERT()` | 🟡 | Limited no-source/`DUAL`/`DO` and single-table row-scalar projection over SQL string/`NULL`, JSON descriptor, and nonbinary string descriptor documents, simple path string/`NULL` literals, and admitted string, signed-64 integer, boolean, `NULL`, JSON descriptor, integer descriptor, boolean-like integer descriptor, and nonbinary string descriptor values; no-source scalar values additionally admit current `JSON_EXTRACT()`, `JSON_ARRAY()`, and `JSON_OBJECT()` results, while row-scalar values admit current `JSON_ARRAY()` and `JSON_OBJECT()` results. Supports existing-path no-op, member insertion, array append, scalar nonzero-index autowrap, duplicate paths left-to-right, missing-intermediate no-op, and root no-op; no stored partial updates, wildcard/range paths, recursive `JSON_INSERT()` values, row-scalar `JSON_EXTRACT()` values, arbitrary expression arguments, predicates, ordering/grouping expressions, DML assignment expressions, binary strings, decimal/float/temporal values, or protocol metadata beyond limited scalar/row-scalar result-column metadata. | [JSON functions and operators](docs/compatibility/functions-json.md), [baseline JSON_INSERT function](docs/specs/baseline-json-insert-function/specs.md) |
| `JSON_KEYS()` | 🟡 | Limited no-source/`DUAL`/`DO` and single-table row-scalar object key arrays over SQL string literals, `NULL`, JSON columns, nonbinary string columns, and supported `JSON_EXTRACT()` results, with optional simple path string literal; no wildcards, multi-match paths, arbitrary expressions, predicates, expression ordering, DML assignment expressions, or protocol metadata beyond limited scalar/row-scalar result-column metadata. | [JSON functions and operators](docs/compatibility/functions-json.md) |
| `JSON_LENGTH()` | 🟡 | Limited no-source/`DUAL`/`DO` and single-table row-scalar shallow length over SQL string literals, `NULL`, JSON columns, nonbinary string columns, and supported `JSON_EXTRACT()` results, with optional simple path string literal; no wildcards, multi-match paths, arbitrary expressions, predicates, expression ordering, DML assignment expressions, full JSON number grammar, or protocol metadata beyond limited scalar/row-scalar result-column metadata. | [JSON functions and operators](docs/compatibility/functions-json.md) |

### System Functions

| Feature | Status | Notes | Full table |
| --- | :-: | --- | --- |
| `DATABASE()` | 🟡 | Limited one-row scalar `SELECT DATABASE()` with optional `FROM DUAL`; returns connection-local selected schema or `NULL`. | [system functions](docs/compatibility/functions-system.md) |
| `SCHEMA()` | 🟡 | Limited synonym for `DATABASE()` in the same scalar-select slice. | [system functions](docs/compatibility/functions-system.md) |
| `CONNECTION_ID()` | 🟡 | Limited one-row scalar select returns a MyLite process-local nonzero handle id; no server thread, process-list, Performance Schema, or `pseudo_thread_id` semantics. | [system functions](docs/compatibility/functions-system.md) |
| `CURRENT_ROLE()` | 🟡 | Limited one-row scalar select returns MyLite's no-active-role value `NONE`; no role catalog, role grants, `SET ROLE`, or bare `CURRENT_ROLE`. | [system functions](docs/compatibility/functions-system.md), [SQL users, roles, and privileges](docs/compatibility/sql-users-privileges.md) |
| `FOUND_ROWS()` | 🟡 | Limited zero-argument one-row scalar select with optional `FROM DUAL` returns connection-local found-row state for supported `SELECT` statements, records deprecation warning 1287 per invocation, and works with the limited `SQL_CALC_FOUND_ROWS` subset; no arguments, table-backed evaluation, protocol metadata, `CLIENT_FOUND_ROWS`, replication semantics, or full diagnostics area behavior. | [system functions](docs/compatibility/functions-system.md) |
| `CURRENT_USER()` / `CURRENT_USER` | 🟡 | Limited one-row scalar select returns MyLite's embedded current identity `root@%`; no account or definer semantics. | [system functions](docs/compatibility/functions-system.md) |
| `LAST_INSERT_ID()` | 🟡 | Limited one-row scalar select and `DO` support for zero-argument reads plus `LAST_INSERT_ID(expr)` with integer, boolean, and `NULL` literal arguments; stores connection-local unsigned 64-bit state shared with the current generated auto-increment insert subset; no table-backed evaluation, DML expression use, warning-producing string/decimal/float conversion, protocol insert-id metadata, C API `mysql_insert_id()`, or stored-program behavior. | [system functions](docs/compatibility/functions-system.md) |
| `ROW_COUNT()` | 🟡 | Limited one-row scalar select with optional result alias returns connection-local row-count state for supported baseline statements; no protocol OK-packet parity, `CLIENT_FOUND_ROWS`, table-backed evaluation, or full diagnostics area support. | [system functions](docs/compatibility/functions-system.md) |
| `SESSION_USER()` | 🟡 | Limited no-whitespace one-row scalar select returns MyLite's embedded client identity `root@%`; no `IGNORE_SPACE`, stored-function resolution, authentication, or host matching. | [system functions](docs/compatibility/functions-system.md) |
| `SYSTEM_USER()` | 🟡 | Limited no-whitespace one-row scalar select returns MyLite's embedded client identity `root@%`; no `SYSTEM_USER` privilege semantics, authentication, or host matching. | [system functions](docs/compatibility/functions-system.md) |
| `USER()` | 🟡 | Limited one-row scalar select returns MyLite's embedded client identity `root@%`; no authentication or host matching. | [system functions](docs/compatibility/functions-system.md) |
| `VERSION()` | 🟡 | Limited one-row scalar select returns the fixed MySQL 8.4.9 compatibility version string; no protocol handshake version reporting or configurable server-version identity. | [system functions](docs/compatibility/functions-system.md), [baseline MySQL server version identity](docs/specs/baseline-mysql-server-version-identity/specs.md) |

| `WEEKOFYEAR()` | 🟡 | Limited no-source, `FROM DUAL`, `DO`, single-table row-scalar projection, and limited single-table numeric extractor predicates for `WEEKOFYEAR(value)` over the current `WEEK()` input subset; equivalent to `WEEK(value, 3)` for the admitted values; invalid string inputs warn 1292. | [temporal functions](docs/compatibility/functions-temporal.md), [SQL query expressions](docs/compatibility/sql-query-expressions.md) |

| `YEARWEEK()` | 🟡 | Limited no-source, `FROM DUAL`, `DO`, single-table row-scalar projection, and limited single-table numeric extractor predicates for `YEARWEEK(value[, mode])` over the current `WEEK()` input subset; mode is limited to signed integer, boolean, or `NULL` literals and is normalized to MySQL's low three bits; omitted mode uses mode 0 and is not affected by `default_week_format`; invalid string inputs warn 1292. | [temporal functions](docs/compatibility/functions-temporal.md), [SQL query expressions](docs/compatibility/sql-query-expressions.md) |

| `INFORMATION_SCHEMA.REFERENTIAL_CONSTRAINTS` | 🟡 | Limited queryable synthetic rows for supported descriptor-owned integer-family foreign keys, with referenced unique constraint name, `MATCH_OPTION = 'NONE'`, and stored `UPDATE_RULE` / `DELETE_RULE` values for the admitted `CASCADE`, `RESTRICT`, `NO ACTION`, and `SET NULL` subset; no `SET DEFAULT`, explicit `MATCH`, recursive action metadata, cross-schema references, views, temporary tables, privileges, or complete MySQL system catalogs. | [INFORMATION_SCHEMA tables](docs/compatibility/metadata-information-schema.md) |
| `INFORMATION_SCHEMA.KEYWORDS` | 🟡 | Queryable synthetic MySQL 8.4.9 keyword catalog with `WORD` and integer `RESERVED` columns, matching system metadata, case-insensitive `WORD` predicates, numeric `RESERVED` predicates, and the current limited information-schema query surface; no parser behavior driven from the table, mutable keyword catalog, bare truth predicates, or full information-schema query support. | [INFORMATION_SCHEMA tables](docs/compatibility/metadata-information-schema.md) |
| `INFORMATION_SCHEMA.OPTIMIZER_TRACE` | 🟡 | Queryable empty synthetic optimizer-trace catalog with MySQL 8.4.9-shaped columns and matching system metadata; no `optimizer_trace` variables, trace collection, JSON trace rows, memory-limit accounting, privilege filtering, or optimizer instrumentation. | [INFORMATION_SCHEMA tables](docs/compatibility/metadata-information-schema.md), [baseline INFORMATION_SCHEMA OPTIMIZER_TRACE](docs/specs/baseline-information-schema-optimizer-trace/specs.md) |

| `INFORMATION_SCHEMA.PARTITIONS` | 🟡 | Limited queryable synthetic partition metadata with one nonpartitioned row per supported persistent base table, one system-view row per supported `information_schema` view, MySQL 8.4.9-shaped columns, fixed nonpartitioned placeholder values, and descriptor-derived table statistics; no partition DDL, partition descriptors, subpartition rows, partition pruning, partition maintenance, temporary-table rows, privileges, or full MySQL system catalogs. | [INFORMATION_SCHEMA tables](docs/compatibility/metadata-information-schema.md) |

| `SHOW COUNT(*) ERRORS` | 🟡 | Limited previous-statement error-condition count with MySQL 8.4.9 column label; counts MyLite's previous error condition only. | [SQL SHOW statements](docs/compatibility/sql-show-statements.md) |
| `SHOW COUNT(*) WARNINGS` | 🟡 | Limited previous-statement diagnostics count with MySQL 8.4.9 column label; counts MyLite previous error conditions and stored warning/note records only, with documented missing-schema `DROP DATABASE IF EXISTS` counted-only result behavior. | [SQL SHOW statements](docs/compatibility/sql-show-statements.md) |

| `SHOW OPEN TABLES` | 🟡 | Limited embedded empty open-table introspection with MySQL 8.4.9 column labels and `LIKE` filters; unknown explicit schemas are empty successes; no table-cache rows, lock counts, name-lock state, temporary tables, `HANDLER`, `WHERE`, privileges, or performance-schema metadata. | [SQL SHOW statements](docs/compatibility/sql-show-statements.md) |
| `SHOW PLUGINS` | 🟡 | Limited one-row `SHOW PLUGINS` result for MyLite's embedded active `InnoDB` storage-engine plugin surface with MySQL 8.4.9 column labels; related `INFORMATION_SCHEMA.PLUGINS` is a separate limited synthetic catalog; no filters, privileges, loadable plugins, disabled plugin rows, alternate engines, or complete MySQL plugin inventory. | [SQL SHOW statements](docs/compatibility/sql-show-statements.md), [SQL components and plugins](docs/compatibility/sql-components-plugins.md), [baseline SHOW PLUGINS metadata](docs/specs/baseline-show-plugins-metadata/specs.md) |
| `SHOW PRIVILEGES` | 🟡 | Limited static MySQL 8.4.9-shaped privilege-name catalog with columns `Privilege`, `Context`, and `Comment` and the observed 73 target-runtime rows; no filters, account storage, grant descriptors, privilege enforcement, mutable dynamic privileges, ordering, or limits. | [SQL SHOW statements](docs/compatibility/sql-show-statements.md), [SQL users, roles, and privileges](docs/compatibility/sql-users-privileges.md), [baseline SHOW PRIVILEGES](docs/specs/baseline-show-privileges/specs.md) |
| `SHOW PROCEDURE STATUS` | 🟡 | Limited empty routine introspection with MySQL 8.4.9 column labels and `LIKE` filters; global and default-schema independent; related empty `INFORMATION_SCHEMA.ROUTINES` metadata is queryable, but there are no routine descriptors, routine rows, routine DDL, `SHOW CREATE PROCEDURE`, `WHERE`, or privileges. | [SQL SHOW statements](docs/compatibility/sql-show-statements.md), [INFORMATION_SCHEMA tables](docs/compatibility/metadata-information-schema.md), [SQL routines](docs/compatibility/sql-routines.md) |
| `SHOW PROCESSLIST` | 🟡 | Limited current embedded-handle row for `SHOW [FULL] PROCESSLIST` with MySQL 8.4.9 columns, selected-schema `db`, `Info` truncation, and default process-list warning count; related limited `INFORMATION_SCHEMA.PROCESSLIST` metadata is queryable, but there are no server-wide threads, privileges, filters, Performance Schema, sys-schema views, or `KILL`. | [SQL SHOW statements](docs/compatibility/sql-show-statements.md), [INFORMATION_SCHEMA tables](docs/compatibility/metadata-information-schema.md) |
| `SHOW STATUS` | 🟡 | Limited runtime-owned status registry with MySQL 8.4.9 columns, optional `GLOBAL`/`SESSION`/`LOCAL` scope, and `LIKE` filters; values are embedded placeholders for current common rows and do not implement live counters, `WHERE`, privileges, Performance Schema, or the full status-variable catalog. | [SQL SHOW statements](docs/compatibility/sql-show-statements.md), [server status variables](docs/compatibility/runtime-status-variables.md) |
| `SHOW TABLES` | 🟡 | Limited descriptor-driven table listing for MyLite user schemas and metadata-only directory listing for built-in `information_schema`, `mysql`, `performance_schema`, and `sys` schemas, with optional `FULL`, `FROM`/`IN` schema, `LIKE` filters, and limited trailing `WHERE` predicates over displayed output columns using string/`NULL` literals, `LIKE`, `REGEXP`/`RLIKE` over the baseline ASCII pattern subset, `IN`, comparisons, `IS NULL`, and boolean connectives; `FULL` reports `BASE TABLE`, `VIEW`, or `SYSTEM VIEW` from descriptors or the built-in directory. No NUL-producing pattern escapes, `EXTENDED`, `LIKE ... WHERE`, `ORDER BY`, `LIMIT`, privileges, temporary-table rows, or queryable unsupported system tables. | [SQL SHOW statements](docs/compatibility/sql-show-statements.md), [baseline SHOW FULL TABLES](docs/specs/baseline-show-full-tables/specs.md), [baseline SHOW TABLES WHERE](docs/specs/baseline-show-tables-where/specs.md), [baseline built-in schema table directory](docs/specs/baseline-built-in-schema-table-directory/specs.md) |
| `SHOW TABLE STATUS` | 🟡 | Limited descriptor-driven persistent base-table and baseline view status rows plus metadata-only built-in schema directory status rows, with MySQL 8.4.9 column labels, schema forms, `LIKE` filters, limited `WHERE` predicates over displayed output columns using string/`NULL` literals, `LIKE`, `REGEXP`/`RLIKE` over the baseline ASCII pattern subset, `IN`, comparisons, `IS NULL`, and boolean connectives. User base rows expose exact physical row counts and descriptor-owned status metadata; built-in rows expose table-directory placeholders and representative MySQL-shaped engine/type/comment/collation fields without live system-table data. No temporary tables, arbitrary `WHERE` expressions, privileges, checksums, full ICU regex semantics, or full storage-engine statistics. | [SQL SHOW statements](docs/compatibility/sql-show-statements.md), [baseline built-in schema table directory](docs/specs/baseline-built-in-schema-table-directory/specs.md) |
| `SHOW TRIGGERS` | 🟡 | Limited schema-resolved empty trigger introspection with MySQL 8.4.9 column labels, optional `FULL`, and `LIKE` filters; related empty `INFORMATION_SCHEMA.TRIGGERS` metadata is queryable, but there are no trigger descriptors, trigger rows, trigger DDL, `SHOW CREATE TRIGGER`, `WHERE`, or privileges. | [SQL SHOW statements](docs/compatibility/sql-show-statements.md), [INFORMATION_SCHEMA tables](docs/compatibility/metadata-information-schema.md) |
| `SHOW VARIABLES` | 🟡 | Limited runtime-owned variable listing for MyLite's current fixed system-variable registry with optional `GLOBAL`/`SESSION`/`LOCAL` scope, `LIKE` filters, and limited `WHERE` predicates over `Variable_name` and `Value`; no full MySQL catalog, arbitrary expressions, warning-producing numeric filter coercions, privileges, Performance Schema, or mutable state. | [SQL SHOW statements](docs/compatibility/sql-show-statements.md), [runtime system variables](docs/compatibility/runtime-system-variables.md) |
| `SHOW WARNINGS` | 🟡 | Limited previous-statement diagnostics rows with MySQL 8.4.9 columns and unsigned decimal `LIMIT` slicing; reports MyLite previous error conditions plus stored warning/note records only, with documented missing-schema `DROP DATABASE IF EXISTS` counted-only result behavior. | [SQL SHOW statements](docs/compatibility/sql-show-statements.md) |
| `DESCRIBE` | 🟡 | Limited table-column introspection alias for the supported `SHOW COLUMNS` subset; no column filters or execution-plan `EXPLAIN` behavior. | [SQL utility statements](docs/compatibility/sql-utility-statements.md) |
| `DESC` | 🟡 | Limited alias for the supported table-only `DESCRIBE` subset. | [SQL utility statements](docs/compatibility/sql-utility-statements.md) |
| `EXPLAIN` for tables | 🟡 | Limited table-column introspection alias for the supported `SHOW COLUMNS` subset; supports only `EXPLAIN table_name`, not column filters, wildcard filters, execution plans, `FORMAT`, `ANALYZE`, or `FOR CONNECTION`. | [SQL utility statements](docs/compatibility/sql-utility-statements.md) |

### Transactions, Locking, and Maintenance

| Feature | Status | Notes | Full table |
| --- | :-: | --- | --- |
| `START TRANSACTION` | 🟡 | Limited explicit user transaction start for current supported MyLite transactional DML, including implicit commit of an active user transaction before a new start, statement-level `READ ONLY` / `READ WRITE`, applying pending `SET TRANSACTION` characteristics, and `WITH CONSISTENT SNAPSHOT` warning behavior; no MVCC snapshot semantics, mutable autocommit, locks, protocol flags, or XA. | [SQL transactions](docs/compatibility/sql-transactions.md) |
| `BEGIN` | 🟡 | Limited alias for `START TRANSACTION`; `BEGIN WORK` is also admitted. | [SQL transactions](docs/compatibility/sql-transactions.md) |
| `BEGIN WORK` | 🟡 | Limited start-transaction synonym. | [SQL transactions](docs/compatibility/sql-transactions.md) |
| `BEGIN IMMEDIATE` | 🟡 | MyLite-specific compatibility extension for SQLite-oriented clients and harnesses; maps to the existing `BEGIN` / `START TRANSACTION` user transaction lifecycle and is not MySQL syntax. No `DEFERRED`, `EXCLUSIVE`, `TRANSACTION`, or other SQLite transaction spellings. | [SQL transactions](docs/compatibility/sql-transactions.md), [baseline BEGIN IMMEDIATE compatibility](docs/specs/baseline-begin-immediate-compatibility/specs.md) |
| `COMMIT` | 🟡 | Limited explicit commit of the active MyLite user transaction, plus no-op success outside a transaction; admits optional `WORK` only. | [SQL transactions](docs/compatibility/sql-transactions.md) |
| `ROLLBACK` | 🟡 | Limited explicit rollback of the active MyLite user transaction, plus no-op success outside a transaction; admits optional `WORK` only. | [SQL transactions](docs/compatibility/sql-transactions.md) |
| `SAVEPOINT` | 🟡 | Limited user-visible savepoint creation and same-name replacement inside current explicit MyLite user transactions; outside a transaction it is a MySQL-compatible no-op success under the fixed autocommit baseline; no stored-program savepoint levels, mutable autocommit, or protocol flags. | [SQL transactions](docs/compatibility/sql-transactions.md) |
| `ROLLBACK TO SAVEPOINT` | 🟡 | Limited partial rollback for current user-visible savepoints, including `ROLLBACK TO`, `ROLLBACK TO SAVEPOINT`, and `ROLLBACK WORK TO [SAVEPOINT]`; missing savepoints return MySQL-compatible error `1305`; no stored-program scoping or lock/protocol semantics. | [SQL transactions](docs/compatibility/sql-transactions.md) |
| `RELEASE SAVEPOINT` | 🟡 | Limited release of current user-visible savepoints and later savepoints in the active explicit transaction, with MySQL-compatible missing-savepoint diagnostics; no stored-program scoping or protocol semantics. | [SQL transactions](docs/compatibility/sql-transactions.md) |
| `SET TRANSACTION` | 🟡 | Limited `SET [SESSION] TRANSACTION` isolation/access characteristics for the current connection; `READ ONLY` rejects persistent-table DML while allowing temporary-table DML; pending values are consumed by `START TRANSACTION` and other transaction-consuming statements; related transaction system variables expose and update the same session and next-transaction state; isolation is tracked without adding new concurrency semantics; no mutable `GLOBAL`, privileges, locks, or protocol flags. | [SQL transactions](docs/compatibility/sql-transactions.md) |
| `LOCK TABLES` | 🟡 | Limited `LOCK TABLE[S] table [AS] alias READ [LOCAL] \| WRITE [, ...]` for persistent and shadowing temporary base tables: resolves target names, records connection-local lock intent, releases prior lock intent, and implicitly commits an active user transaction before success; no cross-session blocking, access enforcement, alias-only access rules, DDL restrictions while locked, implicit trigger/view/foreign-key locks, privileges, or metadata-lock instrumentation. | [SQL locking](docs/compatibility/sql-locking.md) |
| `UNLOCK TABLES` | 🟡 | Limited `UNLOCK TABLE[S]` releases the current connection-local lock intent and returns the MySQL-shaped non-row success surface; no global read lock handling or broader lock enforcement. | [SQL locking](docs/compatibility/sql-locking.md) |
| `ANALYZE TABLE` | 🟡 | Limited persistent and shadowing temporary base-table result-set surface with optional `NO_WRITE_TO_BINLOG` / `LOCAL`, multi-target ordering, MySQL-shaped missing-target rows, implicit user-transaction commit, `ROW_COUNT() = -1`, and no warnings; no histograms, partition maintenance, statistics refresh, optimizer effects, privileges, or binary logging. | [SQL table maintenance](docs/compatibility/sql-table-maintenance.md) |
| `CHECK TABLE` | 🟡 | Limited persistent and shadowing temporary base-table result-set surface with admitted `QUICK`, `FAST`, `MEDIUM`, `EXTENDED`, `CHANGED`, and `FOR UPGRADE` options as no-ops, multi-target ordering, MySQL-shaped missing-target rows, implicit user-transaction commit, `ROW_COUNT() = -1`, and no warnings; no physical integrity checking, partition checks, checksum validation, privileges, or repair advice. | [SQL table maintenance](docs/compatibility/sql-table-maintenance.md) |
| `CHECKSUM TABLE` | 🟡 | Limited result-set surface for persistent and shadowing temporary base tables with optional `QUICK` / `EXTENDED`, multi-target ordering, MySQL-shaped unavailable-target warning diagnostics, implicit user-transaction commit, and `ROW_COUNT() = -1`; checksum values are `NULL` pending MySQL-compatible row-format checksum calculation. | [SQL table maintenance](docs/compatibility/sql-table-maintenance.md), [baseline spec](docs/specs/baseline-checksum-table/specs.md) |
| `OPTIMIZE TABLE` | 🟡 | Limited persistent and shadowing temporary base-table result-set surface with optional `NO_WRITE_TO_BINLOG` / `LOCAL`, InnoDB-style note plus status rows, multi-target ordering, MySQL-shaped missing-target rows, implicit user-transaction commit, `ROW_COUNT() = -1`, and no warnings; no table rebuild, statistics refresh, free-space recovery, optimizer effects, privileges, or binary logging. | [SQL table maintenance](docs/compatibility/sql-table-maintenance.md) |
| `REPAIR TABLE` | 🟡 | Limited persistent and shadowing temporary base-table result-set surface with optional `NO_WRITE_TO_BINLOG` / `LOCAL` and admitted `QUICK`, `EXTENDED`, and `USE_FRM` options as no-ops, InnoDB-style unsupported-repair note rows, multi-target ordering, MySQL-shaped missing-target rows, implicit user-transaction commit, `ROW_COUNT() = -1`, and no warnings; no physical repair, partition repair, `.frm` handling, privileges, or binary logging. | [SQL table maintenance](docs/compatibility/sql-table-maintenance.md) |

## Maintenance rules

- Keep this file as an implementation contract, not a marketing scorecard.
- Split rows when syntax, behavior, metadata, warnings, or side effects need independent tests.
- Document deliberate embedded-design incompatibilities explicitly instead of silently dropping them.
- Keep this baseline tracker, `docs/compatibility/`, feature guides, design notes, and MySQL-runtime tests in sync.
- Prefer MySQL 8.4.9 behavior over convenience, SQLite defaults, or older MySQL/MariaDB behavior.
- Every implemented row needs MySQL 8.4.9 comparison tests.
- Tests must cover results, errors, warnings, metadata, affected rows, inserted ids, session state, and side effects.
- Syntax-only compatibility must still test parser acceptance and exact warning, error, or placeholder behavior.
- Add focused guide documentation when behavior needs design rationale.
- When MySQL behavior depends on platform or storage engine, tests must document the MyLite contract and observed MySQL baseline.
